Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quasars module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 147. The projective resolution has length at most 770 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to quasars structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 147/770 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The quasars operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 971. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 461 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 971 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the quasars structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 461. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
